- [ ] **Step 5: `file-view.slint`'i ızgarayla yeniden yaz**

`crates/gezik/ui/widgets/file-view.slint` (tamamı; `HeaderCell` Task 11'deki gibi kalır, aşağıda değişmeden tekrarlanmaz — dosyada `HeaderCell` bileşenini olduğu gibi bırak ve yalnızca `FileRow`, `ItemRow` ve `FileView`'u aşağıdakilerle değiştir):

```slint
// One entry, as Rust (view/model.rs) builds it for the entries on screen.
export struct FileRow {
    name: string,
    is-dir: bool,
    // `Kind` in gezik-core kind.rs: Gezik's icon and its color.
    kind: int,
    size: string,
    modified: string,
    created: string,
    type-name: string,
    // The system's icon or the thumbnail (`has-icon`), else Gezik's own for `kind`.
    icon: image,
    has-icon: bool,
    selected: bool,
    // Has the keyboard focus; the ring shows only while the view has the keyboard.
    focused: bool,
}

// One line of the view: one entry in the list, a row of cells in the grid.
export struct ItemRow {
    // The index of its first entry.
    first: int,
    cells: [FileRow],
}

// The folder's entries, as a list with columns or a grid. Rust owns the selection: this
// reports presses and draws.
export component FileView inherits Rectangle {
    in property <[ItemRow]> items;
    // 0: list, 1: grid.
    in property <int> mode;
    // The grid's picture size.
    in property <length> picture-size: 96px;
    // The view has the keyboard: the focused entry shows a ring.
    in property <bool> has-focus;
    // Scroll offset (content-y: zero or negative).
    in-out property <length> scroll;
    // Column widths (0: hidden); Rust sets them, a dragged edge changes them here.
    in-out property <length> col-modified;
    in-out property <length> col-created;
    in-out property <length> col-type;
    in-out property <length> col-size;
    // The sort column (0 Name, 1 Modified, 2 Created, 3 Type, 4 Size) and direction.
    in property <int> sort-column;
    in property <bool> sort-desc;
    out property <length> cell-width: max(root.picture-size + 24px, 96px);
    out property <length> cell-height: root.picture-size + 24px + Theme.font-size * 2.8;
    // Grid cells per line.
    out property <int> grid-columns: max(1, floor(list.visible-width / root.cell-width));
    out property <length> item-height: root.mode == 0 ? Theme.row-height : root.cell-height;
    // How many lines PgUp/PgDn move: one screen, less one line.
    out property <int> page-rows: max(1, floor(list.visible-height / self.item-height) - 1);

    // Left press on entry `index`, with Ctrl (Cmd on macOS) and Shift.
    callback item-pressed(int, bool, bool);
    callback item-double-clicked(int);
    callback item-middle-clicked(int);
    // Right-click on an entry, at a window position.
    callback item-menu(int, length, length);
    // Left click on empty space, with Ctrl.
    callback background-pressed(bool);
    callback background-menu(length, length);
    // A press in the view: the keyboard should go to it.
    callback focus-requested();
    // A rubber-band drag: the rectangle in content coordinates (`Rect` in gezik-core
    // layout.rs), on every pointer move; Ctrl adds to the selection the drag started with.
    callback marquee(length, length, length, length, bool);
    callback marquee-done();
    callback header-clicked(int);
    callback header-menu(length, length);
    callback columns-resized();
    callback grid-columns-changed(int);
    // Ctrl+wheel: true for bigger pictures.
    callback zoom(bool);

    changed grid-columns => { root.grid-columns-changed(self.grid-columns); }

    property <int> per-row: root.mode == 0 ? 1 : root.grid-columns;
    property <length> header-height: root.mode == 0 ? Theme.row-height : 0px;
    property <length> gap: Theme.spacing + 2px;
    property <length> side: Theme.spacing + 4px;
    property <int> shown-columns: (root.col-modified > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (root.col-created > 0 ? 1 : 0)
        + (root.col-type > 0 ? 1 : 0) + (root.col-size > 0 ? 1 : 0);
    // Right edge of the Name column: a drag that starts right of it, on a row, selects with
    // a rectangle; from the name it is kept for dragging files (sub-project 4).
    property <length> name-end: root.width - root.side - root.col-modified - root.col-created - root.col-type
        - root.col-size - root.gap * root.shown-columns;

    property <bool> marquee-active;
    property <bool> marquee-additive;
    // Where the drag started, in content coordinates, and where the pointer is, in the
    // list's own coordinates (it stays put while the list scrolls under it).
    property <length> start-x;
    property <length> start-y;
    property <length> pointer-x;
    property <length> pointer-y;

    clip: true;

    // Scrolls so entry `index` is fully visible.
    public function ensure-visible(index: int) {
        if index < 0 {
            return;
        }
        let line = floor(index / root.per-row);
        if line * root.item-height < -root.scroll {
            root.scroll = -line * root.item-height;
        } else if (line + 1) * root.item-height > -root.scroll + list.visible-height {
            root.scroll = -((line + 1) * root.item-height - list.visible-height);
        }
    }

    // Where the keyboard menu of entry `index` opens (a window position): just below it.
    // -1: the background's, at the top left.
    public function menu-x(index: int) -> length {
        if index < 0 {
            return list.absolute-position.x + Theme.spacing * 2;
        }
        if root.mode == 0 {
            return list.absolute-position.x + Theme.spacing * 6;
        }
        return list.absolute-position.x + mod(index, root.per-row) * root.cell-width + root.cell-width / 2;
    }

    public function menu-y(index: int) -> length {
        if index < 0 {
            return list.absolute-position.y + Theme.spacing * 2;
        }
        return list.absolute-position.y + root.scroll + (floor(index / root.per-row) + 1) * root.item-height;
    }

    // `x`, `y`: the press, in the list's coordinates.
    function begin-marquee(x: length, y: length, additive: bool) {
        root.start-x = x;
        root.start-y = y - root.scroll;
        root.pointer-x = x;
        root.pointer-y = y;
        root.marquee-additive = additive;
        root.marquee-active = true;
    }

    function track-marquee(x: length, y: length) {
        root.pointer-x = x;
        root.pointer-y = y;
        let content-y = y - root.scroll;
        root.marquee(min(root.start-x, x), min(root.start-y, content-y), abs(x - root.start-x), abs(content-y - root.start-y), root.marquee-additive);
    }

    function end-marquee() {
        if root.marquee-active {
            root.marquee-active = false;
            root.marquee-done();
        }
    }

    // A move or release seen by any entry or the background while a drag runs: the entry
    // that started it may have scrolled away (the list drops lines off screen) and taken
    // the pointer grab with it.
    function marquee-pointer(kind: PointerEventKind, x: length, y: length) {
        if !root.marquee-active {
            return;
        }
        if kind == PointerEventKind.move {
            root.track-marquee(x, y);
        }
        if kind == PointerEventKind.up || kind == PointerEventKind.cancel {
            root.end-marquee();
        }
    }

    // Ctrl+wheel anywhere in the view; a plain wheel scrolls.
    function wheel(event: PointerScrollEvent) -> EventResult {
        if event.modifiers.control {
            root.zoom(event.delta-y > 0);
            return accept;
        }
        return reject;
    }

    if root.mode == 0: Rectangle {
        x: 0;
        y: 0;
        width: root.width;
        height: root.header-height;
        background: Theme.surface;

        HorizontalLayout {
            padding-left: root.side;
            padding-right: root.side;
            spacing: root.gap;
            HeaderCell {
                horizontal-stretch: 1;
                min-width: 150px;
                text: "Name";
                indent: Theme.icon-size + root.gap;
                resizable: false;
                sorted: root.sort-column == 0;
                descending: root.sort-desc;
                clicked => { root.header-clicked(0); }
                menu(x, y) => { root.header-menu(x, y); }
            }
            if root.col-modified > 0: HeaderCell {
                width: root.col-modified;
                text: "Modified";
                sorted: root.sort-column == 1;
                descending: root.sort-desc;
                clicked => { root.header-clicked(1); }
                menu(x, y) => { root.header-menu(x, y); }
                resize(w) => { root.col-modified = clamp(w, 50px, 600px); }
                resize-done => { root.columns-resized(); }
            }
            if root.col-created > 0: HeaderCell {
                width: root.col-created;
                text: "Created";
                sorted: root.sort-column == 2;
                descending: root.sort-desc;
                clicked => { root.header-clicked(2); }
                menu(x, y) => { root.header-menu(x, y); }
                resize(w) => { root.col-created = clamp(w, 50px, 600px); }
                resize-done => { root.columns-resized(); }
            }
            if root.col-type > 0: HeaderCell {
                width: root.col-type;
                text: "Type";
                sorted: root.sort-column == 3;
                descending: root.sort-desc;
                clicked => { root.header-clicked(3); }
                menu(x, y) => { root.header-menu(x, y); }
                resize(w) => { root.col-type = clamp(w, 50px, 600px); }
                resize-done => { root.columns-resized(); }
            }
            if root.col-size > 0: HeaderCell {
                width: root.col-size;
                text: "Size";
                right-aligned: true;
                sorted: root.sort-column == 4;
                descending: root.sort-desc;
                clicked => { root.header-clicked(4); }
                menu(x, y) => { root.header-menu(x, y); }
                resize(w) => { root.col-size = clamp(w, 50px, 600px); }
                resize-done => { root.columns-resized(); }
            }
        }

        Rectangle {
            y: parent.height - 1px;
            height: 1px;
            background: Theme.border;
        }
    }

    // Behind the entries: presses on empty space (the list passes them through). A drag
    // from here selects with a rectangle; a click clears the selection.
    TouchArea {
        x: list.x;
        y: list.y;
        width: list.width;
        height: list.height;
        property <bool> ctrl;
        scroll-event(event) => { return root.wheel(event); }
        pointer-event(event) => {
            if event.kind == PointerEventKind.down && event.button == PointerEventButton.left {
                self.ctrl = event.modifiers.control;
                root.focus-requested();
            }
            if event.kind == PointerEventKind.up && event.button == PointerEventButton.left {
                if root.marquee-active {
                    root.end-marquee();
                } else {
                    root.background-pressed(self.ctrl);
                }
            }
            if event.kind == PointerEventKind.up && event.button == PointerEventButton.right {
                root.background-menu(self.absolute-position.x + self.mouse-x, self.absolute-position.y + self.mouse-y);
            }
            if event.kind == PointerEventKind.move && !self.pressed {
                root.marquee-pointer(event.kind, self.mouse-x, self.mouse-y);
            }
        }
        moved => {
            if !root.marquee-active && (abs(self.mouse-x - self.pressed-x) > 4px || abs(self.mouse-y - self.pressed-y) > 4px) {
                root.begin-marquee(self.pressed-x, self.pressed-y, self.ctrl);
            }
            if root.marquee-active {
                root.track-marquee(self.mouse-x, self.mouse-y);
            }
        }
    }

    // ListView only instantiates the lines on screen, so huge folders stay cheap.
    list := ListView {
        x: 0;
        y: root.header-height;
        width: root.width;
        height: root.height - root.header-height;
        content-y <=> root.scroll;
        for item in root.items: line := Rectangle {
            height: root.item-height;

            // The list: one entry with its columns.
            if root.mode == 0: entry := Rectangle {
                property <FileRow> row: item.cells[0];
                property <int> index: item.first;
                property <color> text-color: self.row.selected ? Theme.selection-foreground : Theme.foreground;
                property <color> muted-color: self.row.selected ? Theme.selection-foreground : Theme.foreground-muted;
                background: self.row.selected ? Theme.selection : touch.has-hover ? Theme.hover : transparent;
                accessible-role: list-item;
                accessible-label: self.row.name;
                accessible-item-selectable: true;
                accessible-item-selected: self.row.selected;
                accessible-item-index: self.index;

                touch := TouchArea {
                    property <bool> ctrl;
                    double-clicked => { root.item-double-clicked(entry.index); }
                    scroll-event(event) => { return root.wheel(event); }
                    pointer-event(event) => {
                        if event.kind == PointerEventKind.down && event.button == PointerEventButton.left {
                            self.ctrl = event.modifiers.control;
                            root.focus-requested();
                            root.item-pressed(entry.index, event.modifiers.control, event.modifiers.shift);
                        }
                        if event.kind == PointerEventKind.up && event.button == PointerEventButton.middle {
                            root.item-middle-clicked(entry.index);
                        }
                        if event.kind == PointerEventKind.up && event.button == PointerEventButton.right {
                            root.item-menu(entry.index, self.absolute-position.x + self.mouse-x, self.absolute-position.y + self.mouse-y);
                        }
                        if !self.pressed || event.kind == PointerEventKind.up {
                            root.marquee-pointer(event.kind, self.mouse-x, line.y + root.scroll + self.mouse-y);
                        }
                    }
                    moved => {
                        if !root.marquee-active && self.pressed-x > root.name-end
                            && (abs(self.mouse-x - self.pressed-x) > 4px || abs(self.mouse-y - self.pressed-y) > 4px) {
                            root.begin-marquee(self.pressed-x, line.y + root.scroll + self.pressed-y, self.ctrl);
                        }
                        if root.marquee-active {
                            root.track-marquee(self.mouse-x, line.y + root.scroll + self.mouse-y);
                        }
                    }
                }

                HorizontalLayout {
                    padding-left: root.side;
                    padding-right: root.side;
                    spacing: root.gap;
                    HorizontalLayout {
                        horizontal-stretch: 1;
                        min-width: 150px;
                        spacing: root.gap;
                        VerticalLayout {
                            alignment: center;
                            FileIcon {
                                width: Theme.icon-size;
                                height: Theme.icon-size;
                                kind: entry.row.kind;
                                picture: entry.row.icon;
                                has-picture: entry.row.has-icon;
                            }
                        }
                        Text {
                            text: entry.row.name;
                            vertical-alignment: center;
                            horizontal-stretch: 1;
                            overflow: elide;
                            color: entry.text-color;
                        }
                    }
                    if root.col-modified > 0: Text {
                        width: root.col-modified;
                        text: entry.row.modified;
                        vertical-alignment: center;
                        overflow: elide;
                        color: entry.muted-color;
                    }
                    if root.col-created > 0: Text {
                        width: root.col-created;
                        text: entry.row.created;
                        vertical-alignment: center;
                        overflow: elide;
                        color: entry.muted-color;
                    }
                    if root.col-type > 0: Text {
                        width: root.col-type;
                        text: entry.row.type-name;
                        vertical-alignment: center;
                        overflow: elide;
                        color: entry.muted-color;
                    }
                    if root.col-size > 0: Text {
                        width: root.col-size;
                        text: entry.row.size;
                        horizontal-alignment: right;
                        vertical-alignment: center;
                        overflow: elide;
                        color: entry.muted-color;
                    }
                }

                if entry.row.focused && root.has-focus: Rectangle {
                    border-width: 1px;
                    border-color: Theme.focus-ring;
                }
            }

            // The grid: a row of cells. The gaps between them let presses through to the
            // background, where a drag selects with a rectangle.
            if root.mode == 1: Rectangle {
                for cell[c] in item.cells: tile := Rectangle {
                    property <int> index: item.first + c;
                    x: c * root.cell-width;
                    width: root.cell-width;
                    height: root.cell-height;
                    accessible-role: list-item;
                    accessible-label: cell.name;
                    accessible-item-selectable: true;
                    accessible-item-selected: cell.selected;
                    accessible-item-index: self.index;

                    Rectangle {
                        x: 4px;
                        y: 4px;
                        width: parent.width - 8px;
                        height: parent.height - 8px;
                        border-radius: Theme.radius;
                        background: cell.selected ? Theme.selection : tile-touch.has-hover ? Theme.hover : transparent;
                        border-width: cell.focused && root.has-focus ? 1px : 0px;
                        border-color: Theme.focus-ring;
                    }

                    FileIcon {
                        x: (parent.width - self.width) / 2;
                        y: 10px;
                        width: root.picture-size;
                        height: root.picture-size;
                        kind: cell.kind;
                        picture: cell.icon;
                        has-picture: cell.has-icon;
                    }

                    Text {
                        x: 8px;
                        y: 14px + root.picture-size;
                        width: parent.width - 16px;
                        height: parent.height - self.y - 8px;
                        text: cell.name;
                        wrap: word-wrap;
                        overflow: elide;
                        horizontal-alignment: center;
                        vertical-alignment: top;
                        color: cell.selected ? Theme.selection-foreground : Theme.foreground;
                    }

                    tile-touch := TouchArea {
                        x: 4px;
                        y: 4px;
                        width: parent.width - 8px;
                        height: parent.height - 8px;
                        double-clicked => { root.item-double-clicked(tile.index); }
                        scroll-event(event) => { return root.wheel(event); }
                        pointer-event(event) => {
                            if event.kind == PointerEventKind.down && event.button == PointerEventButton.left {
                                root.focus-requested();
                                root.item-pressed(tile.index, event.modifiers.control, event.modifiers.shift);
                            }
                            if event.kind == PointerEventKind.up && event.button == PointerEventButton.middle {
                                root.item-middle-clicked(tile.index);
                            }
                            if event.kind == PointerEventKind.up && event.button == PointerEventButton.right {
                                root.item-menu(tile.index, self.absolute-position.x + self.mouse-x, self.absolute-position.y + self.mouse-y);
                            }
                            if !self.pressed || event.kind == PointerEventKind.up {
                                root.marquee-pointer(event.kind, tile.x + self.x + self.mouse-x, line.y + root.scroll + self.y + self.mouse-y);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Near the top or bottom edge, a running drag scrolls the list.
    Timer {
        interval: 30ms;
        running: root.marquee-active && (root.pointer-y < 24px || root.pointer-y > list.visible-height - 24px);
        triggered => {
            let step = root.item-height / 2;
            let lowest = min(0px, list.visible-height - list.content-height);
            root.scroll = clamp(root.pointer-y < 24px ? root.scroll + step : root.scroll - step, lowest, 0px);
            root.track-marquee(root.pointer-x, root.pointer-y);
        }
    }

    if root.marquee-active: Rectangle {
        property <length> top: root.start-y + root.scroll;
        x: list.x + min(root.start-x, root.pointer-x);
        y: list.y + min(self.top, root.pointer-y);
        width: abs(root.pointer-x - root.start-x);
        height: abs(root.pointer-y - self.top);
        background: Theme.marquee;
        border-width: 1px;
        border-color: Theme.focus-ring;
    }
}
```

(Not: başlık şeridi artık `if root.mode == 0:` ile koşullu; liste `y: root.header-height`'ten başlar. Slint `floor()` tam sayı verir; `mod()` tam sayılarla çalışır.)

`app.slint`'te `AppWindow`'a:

```slint
    // 0: list, 1: grid; the grid's picture size.
    in property <int> view-mode;
    in property <length> grid-size: 96px;
    out property <length> cell-width: file-view.cell-width;
    out property <length> cell-height: file-view.cell-height;
    out property <int> grid-columns: file-view.grid-columns;
    callback grid-columns-changed(int);
    // Ctrl+wheel in the view: true for bigger.
    callback zoom(bool);
    // The View button, with the window position for its menu.
    callback view-menu(length, length);
```

`file-view := FileView { … }` içine:

```slint
                    mode: root.view-mode;
                    picture-size: root.grid-size;
                    grid-columns-changed(n) => { root.grid-columns-changed(n); }
                    zoom(bigger) => { root.zoom(bigger); }
```

Araç çubuğunda `address := Breadcrumb { … }`'ten sonra:

```slint
                    ToolButton {
                        text: "View";
                        // Four squares.
                        icon: "M 2 2 L 7 2 L 7 7 L 2 7 Z M 9 2 L 14 2 L 14 7 L 9 7 Z M 2 9 L 7 9 L 7 14 L 2 14 Z M 9 9 L 14 9 L 14 14 L 9 14 Z";
                        clicked => {
                            root.end-path-editing();
                            root.view-menu(self.absolute-position.x, self.absolute-position.y + self.height);
                        }
                    }
```

- Modify: `crates/gezik/ui/app.slint`, `crates/gezik/src/view/mod.rs`, `crates/gezik/src/context_menu.rs`, `crates/gezik/src/main.rs`

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:

- [ ] **Step 1: `preview.rs`'nin saf kısmını testleriyle yaz**

```rust
//! The preview of the selection: the pane on the right (Alt+P) and the quick look window
//! (Space) show the same thing. Loading runs on a short-lived thread per request; a newer
//! request makes older results be dropped.

use std::cell::{Cell, RefCell};
use std::io::Read;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::rc::Rc;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::time::{Duration, SystemTime};

use gezik_core::format_size;
use gezik_platform::{IconTarget, Rgba, format_datetime};
use slint::{ComponentHandle, Image, Rgba8Pixel, SharedPixelBuffer};

use crate::view::View;
use crate::{AppWindow, PreviewInfo};

/// How much of a text file is shown.
pub const TEXT_BYTES: usize = 64 * 1024;
/// A NUL byte in this much of the start makes a file binary, not text.
pub const BINARY_PROBE: usize = 8 * 1024;
/// A folder's entries are counted up to this many.
pub const MAX_COUNTED: usize = 10_000;

/// What to preview, captured on the UI thread.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Target {
    Nothing,
    Entry {
        path: PathBuf,
        is_dir: bool,
        name: String,
        type_name: String,
        /// Files only.
        size: Option<u64>,
        modified: Option<SystemTime>,
        created: Option<SystemTime>,
        /// `Kind` index, for the fallback icon.
        kind: i32,
    },
    Several {
        count: usize,
        /// Of the selected files; `None` if only folders are selected.
        size: Option<u64>,
    },
}

/// What the loading thread found; `Send`.
pub enum Body {
    None,
    /// The picture, and its own size when it is the file itself (not a thumbnail or icon).
    Picture(SharedPixelBuffer<Rgba8Pixel>, Option<(u32, u32)>),
    Text(String),
    Folder { count: usize, more: bool },
}

fn buffer(rgba: Rgba) -> Option<SharedPixelBuffer<Rgba8Pixel>> {
    let expected = rgba.width as usize * rgba.height as usize * 4;
    (rgba.width > 0 && rgba.pixels.len() == expected)
        .then(|| SharedPixelBuffer::clone_from_slice(&rgba.pixels, rgba.width, rgba.height))
}

/// Loads the preview of `target`, pictures fitting `px`. Runs on a worker thread; never
/// panics on bad files.
pub fn load(target: &Target, px: u32) -> Body {
    let Target::Entry { path, is_dir, .. } = target else { return Body::None };
    if *is_dir {
        let (count, more) = count_entries(path);
        return Body::Folder { count, more };
    }
    let ext = path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()).unwrap_or_default().to_lowercase();
    if gezik_platform::can_decode(&ext)
        && let Ok(decoded) = gezik_platform::decode_image(path, px)
        && let Some(picture) = buffer(decoded.image)
    {
        return Body::Picture(picture, Some((decoded.width, decoded.height)));
    }
    if let Some(text) = read_text(path) {
        return Body::Text(text);
    }
    let picture = gezik_platform::thumbnail(path, px).or_else(|| gezik_platform::icon(&IconTarget::Path(path.clone()), px));
    match picture.and_then(buffer) {
        Some(picture) => Body::Picture(picture, None),
        None => Body::None,
    }
}

/// The start of a text file (at most [`TEXT_BYTES`]); `None` for binary files (a NUL in
/// the first [`BINARY_PROBE`] bytes) and unreadable ones.
pub fn read_text(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
    let file = std::fs::File::open(path).ok()?;
    let mut bytes = Vec::with_capacity(TEXT_BYTES);
    file.take(TEXT_BYTES as u64).read_to_end(&mut bytes).ok()?;
    if bytes[..bytes.len().min(BINARY_PROBE)].contains(&0) {
        return None;
    }
    Some(String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes).into_owned())
}

/// How many entries a folder has, up to [`MAX_COUNTED`]; `true` if there are more.
pub fn count_entries(path: &Path) -> (usize, bool) {
    let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(path) else { return (0, false) };
    let count = entries.take(MAX_COUNTED + 1).count();
    (count.min(MAX_COUNTED), count > MAX_COUNTED)
}

/// What the pane shows for `target`; `body` is `None` while it loads.
pub fn describe(target: &Target, body: Option<Body>) -> PreviewInfo {
    match target {
        Target::Nothing => PreviewInfo::default(),
        Target::Several { count, size } => PreviewInfo {
            kind: 5,
            title: format!("{count} items selected").into(),
            details: size.map(|s| format!("Total size: {}", format_size(s))).unwrap_or_default().into(),
            item_kind: 1,
            ..PreviewInfo::default()
        },
        Target::Entry { name, type_name, size, modified, created, kind, is_dir, .. } => {
            let mut lines = vec![type_name.clone()];
            if let Some(size) = size {
                lines.push(format_size(*size));
            }
            if let Some(time) = modified {
                lines.push(format!("Modified {}", format_datetime(*time)));
            }
            if let Some(time) = created {
                lines.push(format!("Created {}", format_datetime(*time)));
            }
            let mut info = PreviewInfo {
                kind: if *is_dir { 4 } else { 3 },
                title: name.as_str().into(),
                item_kind: *kind,
                loading: body.is_none(),
                ..PreviewInfo::default()
            };
            match body {
                None | Some(Body::None) => {}
                Some(Body::Picture(picture, size)) => {
                    if let Some((w, h)) = size {
                        lines.push(format!("{w} × {h} pixels"));
                        info.kind = 1;
                    }
                    info.picture = Image::from_rgba8(picture);
                    info.has_picture = true;
                }
                Some(Body::Text(text)) => {
                    info.kind = 2;
                    info.text = text.into();
                }
                Some(Body::Folder { count, more }) => lines.push(match (count, more) {
                    (_, true) => format!("{MAX_COUNTED}+ items"),
                    (1, false) => "1 item".to_owned(),
                    (n, false) => format!("{n} items"),
                }),
            }
            info.details = lines.join("\n").into();
            info
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn temp(name: &str) -> PathBuf {
        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("gezik-preview-{name}-{}", std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
        dir
    }

    fn entry(path: PathBuf, is_dir: bool) -> Target {
        Target::Entry {
            name: path.file_name().unwrap().to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
            path,
            is_dir,
            type_name: "Thing".into(),
            size: (!is_dir).then_some(2048),
            modified: None,
            created: None,
            kind: 1,
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn text_files_show_their_start() {
        let dir = temp("text");
        let path = dir.join("a.txt");
        std::fs::write(&path, "merhaba dünya").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(read_text(&path).as_deref(), Some("merhaba dünya"));
        let big = dir.join("big.log");
        std::fs::write(&big, "x".repeat(TEXT_BYTES * 2)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(read_text(&big).unwrap().len(), TEXT_BYTES);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
    }

    #[test]
    fn binary_files_are_not_text() {
        let dir = temp("binary");
        let path = dir.join("a.bin");
        std::fs::write(&path, b"MZ\x90\x00\x03\x00").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(read_text(&path), None);
        assert_eq!(read_text(&dir.join("missing")), None);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
    }

    #[test]
    fn folders_are_counted_up_to_the_limit() {
        let dir = temp("count");
        for i in 0..3 {
            std::fs::write(dir.join(format!("{i}")), "").unwrap();
        }
        assert_eq!(count_entries(&dir), (3, false));
        assert_eq!(count_entries(&dir.join("missing")), (0, false));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
    }

    #[test]
    fn loads_pictures_with_their_size() {
        let dir = temp("picture");
        let path = dir.join("p.png");
        image::RgbaImage::from_pixel(300, 200, image::Rgba([1, 2, 3, 255])).save(&path).unwrap();
        match load(&entry(path, false), 100) {
            Body::Picture(picture, Some((300, 200))) => assert_eq!((picture.width(), picture.height()), (100, 67)),
            _ => panic!("expected the picture"),
        }
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
    }

    #[test]
    fn describes_entries_and_selections() {
        let several = describe(&Target::Several { count: 3, size: Some(1536) }, None);
        assert_eq!((several.kind, several.title.as_str(), several.details.as_str()), (5, "3 items selected", "Total size: 1.5 KB"));
        let file = entry(PathBuf::from("/x/notes.txt"), false);
        let loading = describe(&file, None);
        assert!(loading.loading && loading.kind == 3);
        assert_eq!(loading.details.as_str(), "Thing\n2.0 KB");
        let text = describe(&file, Some(Body::Text("hi".into())));
        assert_eq!((text.kind, text.text.as_str(), text.loading), (2, "hi", false));
        let picture = describe(&file, Some(Body::Picture(SharedPixelBuffer::new(2, 2), Some((300, 200)))));
        assert_eq!(picture.kind, 1);
        assert!(picture.has_picture && picture.details.ends_with("300 × 200 pixels"));
        let folder = describe(&entry(PathBuf::from("/x/sub"), true), Some(Body::Folder { count: MAX_COUNTED, more: true }));
        assert_eq!(folder.kind, 4);
        assert!(folder.details.ends_with("10000+ items"), "{}", folder.details);
        assert_eq!(describe(&Target::Nothing, None).kind, 0);
    }
}
```

```slint
import { Theme } from "../theme.slint";
import { FileIcon } from "file-icon.slint";

// What the preview shows; built by Rust (preview.rs).
export struct PreviewInfo {
    // 0 nothing selected, 1 a picture, 2 text, 3 another file (its thumbnail or icon),
    // 4 a folder, 5 several items.
    kind: int,
    title: string,
    picture: image,
    has-picture: bool,
    text: string,
    // Lines of facts: type, size, dates, picture size, folder count.
    details: string,
    // `Kind` index, for the icon when there is no picture.
    item-kind: int,
    // The picture or text is still loading.
    loading: bool,
}

export component PreviewPane inherits Rectangle {
    in property <PreviewInfo> info;
    // A fixed-width font for text files.
    in property <string> mono-font;

    background: Theme.surface;
    accessible-role: none;

    VerticalLayout {
        padding: Theme.spacing * 2;
        spacing: Theme.spacing * 2;

        Rectangle {
            vertical-stretch: 1;
            min-height: 80px;

            if root.info.kind == 2: Flickable {
                content-height: text.preferred-height;
                content-width: self.width;
                text := Text {
                    width: parent.width;
                    text: root.info.text;
                    font-family: root.mono-font;
                    wrap: word-wrap;
                    color: Theme.foreground;
                }
            }

            if root.info.kind != 2 && root.info.has-picture: Image {
                width: parent.width;
                height: parent.height;
                source: root.info.picture;
                image-fit: contain;
                accessible-role: image;
                accessible-label: root.info.title;
            }

            if root.info.kind != 0 && root.info.kind != 2 && !root.info.has-picture: FileIcon {
                width: min(parent.width, parent.height, 96px);
                height: self.width;
                x: (parent.width - self.width) / 2;
                y: (parent.height - self.height) / 2;
                kind: root.info.kind == 4 ? 0 : root.info.item-kind;
            }

            if root.info.kind == 0: Text {
                width: parent.width;
                height: parent.height;
                text: "Select a file to preview";

                vertical-alignment: center;
                color: Theme.foreground-muted;
            }

        Text {
            text: root.info.title;
            font-weight: 600;
            wrap: word-wrap;
            color: Theme.foreground;
        }

        Text {
            text: root.info.details;
            wrap: word-wrap;
            color: Theme.foreground-muted;
        }
    }
}
```

`preview.rs`'ye (testlerden önce):

```rust
thread_local! {
    /// The preview of this (UI) thread, so loading threads and timers can reach it.
    static CURRENT: RefCell<Option<Preview>> = const { RefCell::new(None) };
}

/// Runs `f` with this UI thread's preview, if there is one.
pub fn with_current<R>(f: impl FnOnce(&Preview) -> R) -> Option<R> {
    let preview = CURRENT.with(|c| c.borrow().clone())?;
    Some(f(&preview))
}

/// How long the selection must stay before its preview loads (arrow keys held down).
const DELAY: Duration = Duration::from_millis(100);

struct Inner {
    window: slint::Weak<AppWindow>,
    view: View,
    pane_open: Cell<bool>,
    /// Bumped per request: a load whose number is no longer current is dropped.
    generation: Arc<AtomicU64>,
    timer: slint::Timer,
    /// What is shown now (the quick look window opens with it).
    info: RefCell<PreviewInfo>,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct Preview(Rc<Inner>);

impl Preview {
    pub fn new(window: &AppWindow, view: View) -> Preview {
        let preview = Preview(Rc::new(Inner {
            window: window.as_weak(),
            view: view.clone(),
            pane_open: Cell::new(false),
            generation: Arc::default(),
            timer: slint::Timer::default(),
            info: RefCell::new(PreviewInfo::default()),
        }));
        CURRENT.with(|c| *c.borrow_mut() = Some(preview.clone()));
        view.on_selection_changed(|| {
            with_current(Preview::schedule);
        });
        preview
    }

    pub fn is_pane_open(&self) -> bool {
        self.0.pane_open.get()
    }

    pub fn set_pane_open(&self, open: bool) {
        self.0.pane_open.set(open);
        if let Some(window) = self.0.window.upgrade() {
            window.set_preview_open(open);
        }
        if open {
            self.refresh();
        } else if !self.active() {
            self.release();
        }
    }

    pub fn toggle_pane(&self) {
        self.set_pane_open(!self.is_pane_open());
    }

    /// Whether anything shows the preview.
    fn active(&self) -> bool {
        self.0.pane_open.get()
    }

    /// The selection changed: loads its preview once it stays for a moment.
    pub fn schedule(&self) {
        if !self.active() {
            return;
        }
        self.0.timer.start(slint::TimerMode::SingleShot, DELAY, || {
            with_current(Preview::refresh);
        });
    }

    /// Shows the selection's facts now and loads its picture or text in the background.
    pub fn refresh(&self) {
        let target = self.0.view.preview_target();
        let generation = self.0.generation.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst) + 1;
        self.publish(describe(&target, None));
        if !matches!(target, Target::Entry { .. }) {
            return;
        }
        let (current, weak, px) = (self.0.generation.clone(), self.0.window.clone(), self.picture_px());
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            gezik_platform::init_thread();
            let body = load(&target, px);
            let _ = weak.upgrade_in_event_loop(move |_| {
                if current.load(Ordering::SeqCst) == generation {
                    with_current(|p| p.publish(describe(&target, Some(body))));
                }
            });
        });
    }

    /// The picture size to load, in physical pixels: what the pane can show.
    fn picture_px(&self) -> u32 {
        let Some(window) = self.0.window.upgrade() else { return 256 };
        let logical = window.get_preview_width();
        (logical * window.window().scale_factor()).round().clamp(64.0, 1024.0) as u32
    }

    fn publish(&self, info: PreviewInfo) {
        if let Some(window) = self.0.window.upgrade() {
            window.set_preview(info.clone());
        }
        *self.0.info.borrow_mut() = info;
    }

    /// Nothing shows the preview: lets go of its picture and drops loads in flight.
    fn release(&self) {
        self.0.generation.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
        self.publish(PreviewInfo::default());
    }
}
```

`view/mod.rs`'ye:

```rust
    /// What the preview shows: several selected items, or the selected entry (the focused
    /// one if it is selected).
    pub fn preview_target(&self) -> crate::preview::Target {
        use crate::preview::Target;
        let data = self.0.data.borrow();
        let count = data.selection.count();
        if count > 1 {
            let mut size = None;
            for i in data.selection.iter().filter(|i| !data.listing.is_dir(*i)) {
                *size.get_or_insert(0) += data.listing.file_size(i);
            }
            return Target::Several { count, size };
        }
        let index = match data.selection.focus() {
            Some(f) if data.selection.is_selected(f) => f,
            _ => match data.selection.iter().next() {
                Some(i) => i,
                None => return Target::Nothing,
            },
        };
        let Some((path, is_dir)) = data.listing.path_at(index) else { return Target::Nothing };
        let entry = match &data.listing {
            Listing::Files(_, entries) => entries.get(index),
            Listing::Drives(_) => None,
        };
        Target::Entry {
            name: data.listing.name_at(index).unwrap_or_default().to_owned(),
            path,
            is_dir,
            type_name: model::type_name_for(&data, index),
            size: (entry.is_some() && !is_dir).then(|| data.listing.file_size(index)),
            modified: entry.and_then(|e| e.modified),
            created: entry.and_then(|e| e.created),
            kind: data.listing.kind(index).index(),
        }
    }
```

```rust
    /// Whether a name is being typed: a key came within the last second. Space then
    /// belongs to the name, not to quick look.
    pub fn is_active(&self, now: Instant) -> bool {
        !self.typed.is_empty() && self.last.is_some_and(|last| now.saturating_duration_since(last) <= Self::WINDOW)
    }
```

`theme_bridge.rs`:

```rust
use std::cell::RefCell;

thread_local! {
    /// The theme on screen, for windows opened later (quick look).
    static CURRENT: RefCell<Option<ResolvedTheme>> = const { RefCell::new(None) };
}

/// The theme on screen now.
pub fn current() -> Option<ResolvedTheme> {
    CURRENT.with(|c| c.borrow().clone())
}

/// Shows `theme` in the main window and every other open Gezik window.
pub fn apply(window: &AppWindow, theme: &ResolvedTheme) {
    apply_global(&window.global::<Theme>(), theme);
    CURRENT.with(|c| *c.borrow_mut() = Some(theme.clone()));
    crate::preview::with_current(|p| p.retheme(theme));
}

/// No validation here: `gezik-config` guarantees every value is present and in range.
pub fn apply_global(global: &Theme<'_>, theme: &ResolvedTheme) {
    // (the former body of `apply`, from `let c = &theme.colors;` on, using `global`)
}
```

(Eski `apply` gövdesindeki `let global = window.global::<Theme>();` satırı kalkar; geri kalan `global.set_…` satırları `apply_global`'a taşınır.)

- [ ] **Step 3: Slint penceresi ve `quick_look.rs`**

`app.slint`'in sonuna:

```slint
// Space on the file list: the selection, big. Space or Esc closes it; the arrow keys move
// in the list underneath.
export component QuickLookWindow inherits Window {
    in property <PreviewInfo> info;
    in property <string> mono-font;
    title: root.info.title == "" ? "Gezik" : root.info.title + " — Gezik";
    background: Theme.background;
    default-font-family: Theme.font-family;
    default-font-size: Theme.font-size;
    // Every key press; returns whether it was used.
    callback key(KeyEvent) -> bool;

    init => { scope.focus(); }

    scope := FocusScope {
        key-pressed(event) => {
            if root.key(event) {
                return accept;
            }
            return reject;
        }
        PreviewPane {
            info: root.info;
            mono-font: root.mono-font;
        }
    }
}
```

`crates/gezik/src/quick_look.rs`:

```rust
//! The quick look window (Space): the selection, big, in its own window over Gezik's.

use gezik_config::theme::ResolvedTheme;
use slint::ComponentHandle;

use crate::{AppWindow, PreviewInfo, QuickLookWindow, Theme};

/// The share of the main window's size it takes.
const SHARE: f32 = 0.7;

pub struct QuickLook {
    window: QuickLookWindow,
}

impl QuickLook {
    /// Opens centered over `main`, showing `info`. `on_key` gets every key press (text and
    /// Ctrl, Alt, Shift, Meta) and returns whether it used it; `on_close` runs when the
    /// window's close button is pressed.
    pub fn open(
        main: &AppWindow,
        info: PreviewInfo,
        mono_font: slint::SharedString,
        on_key: impl Fn(&str, bool, bool, bool, bool) -> bool + 'static,
        on_close: impl Fn() + 'static,
    ) -> Result<QuickLook, slint::PlatformError> {
        let window = QuickLookWindow::new()?;
        if let Some(theme) = crate::theme_bridge::current() {
            crate::theme_bridge::apply_global(&window.global::<Theme>(), &theme);
        }
        window.set_info(info);
        window.set_mono_font(mono_font);
        window.on_key(move |event| {
            let m = event.modifiers;
            on_key(&event.text, m.control, m.alt, m.shift, m.meta)
        });
        window.window().on_close_requested(move || {
            on_close();
            slint::CloseRequestResponse::HideWindow
        });
        let main_window = main.window();
        let scale = main_window.scale_factor();
        let size = main_window.size().to_logical(scale);
        let (width, height) = (size.width * SHARE, size.height * SHARE);
        window.window().set_size(slint::LogicalSize::new(width, height));
        let origin = main_window.position();
        window.window().set_position(slint::PhysicalPosition::new(
            origin.x + ((size.width - width) * scale / 2.0) as i32,
            origin.y + ((size.height - height) * scale / 2.0) as i32,
        ));
        window.show()?;
        Ok(QuickLook { window })
    }

    pub fn set_info(&self, info: PreviewInfo) {
        self.window.set_info(info);
    }

    pub fn retheme(&self, theme: &ResolvedTheme) {
        crate::theme_bridge::apply_global(&self.window.global::<Theme>(), theme);
    }

    /// Width in logical pixels.
    pub fn width(&self) -> f32 {
        let native = self.window.window();
        native.size().to_logical(native.scale_factor()).width
    }

    pub fn close(self) {
        let _ = self.window.hide();
    }
}
```

`main.rs`'ye `mod quick_look;` ekle.

- Yeni metodlar:

```rust
    pub fn quick_look_open(&self) -> bool {
        self.0.quick_look.borrow().is_some()
    }

    /// Space on the list: opens quick look, or closes it.
    pub fn toggle_quick_look(&self) {
        if self.quick_look_open() {
            return self.close_quick_look();
        }
        let Some(window) = self.0.window.upgrade() else { return };
        let opened = crate::quick_look::QuickLook::open(
            &window,
            self.0.info.borrow().clone(),
            window.get_mono_font(),
            |text, ctrl, alt, shift, meta| with_current(|p| p.quick_look_key(text, ctrl, alt, shift, meta)).unwrap_or(false),
            || Preview::close_later(),
        );
        match opened {
            Ok(quick_look) => {
                *self.0.quick_look.borrow_mut() = Some(quick_look);
                self.refresh();
            }
            Err(err) => window.set_status(format!("Cannot open quick look: {err}").into()),
        }
    }

    pub fn close_quick_look(&self) {
        if let Some(quick_look) = self.0.quick_look.borrow_mut().take() {
            quick_look.close();
        }
        if !self.active() {
            self.release();
        }
    }

    /// Closes quick look once the current event is done: its window may be the one
    /// handling it.
    fn close_later() {
        slint::Timer::single_shot(Duration::ZERO, || {
            with_current(Preview::close_quick_look);
        });
    }

    /// A key in the quick look window: Space or Esc closes it, arrows move in the list.
    fn quick_look_key(&self, text: &str, ctrl: bool, alt: bool, shift: bool, meta: bool) -> bool {
        use gezik_config::shortcuts::{Key, Platform};
        use gezik_core::layout::Move;
        let Some(chord) = crate::keys::chord_from_slint(text, ctrl, alt, shift, meta, Platform::current()) else {
            return false;
        };
        if chord.ctrl || chord.alt || chord.meta || chord.shift {
            return false;
        }
        let mv = match chord.key {
            Key::Space | Key::Escape => {
                Preview::close_later();
                return true;
            }
            Key::Up => Move::Up,
            Key::Down => Move::Down,
            Key::Left => Move::Left,
            Key::Right => Move::Right,
            _ => return false,
        };
        self.0.view.key_move(mv, false, false, 1)
    }

    /// The theme changed: quick look follows (the main window is done by theme_bridge).
    pub fn retheme(&self, theme: &gezik_config::theme::ResolvedTheme) {
        if let Some(quick_look) = self.0.quick_look.borrow().as_ref() {
            quick_look.retheme(theme);
        }
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Izgara bellek betiğini yaz**

`scripts/perf/grid.ps1`:

```powershell
# Memory with the grid view and thumbnails, in a folder of pictures. Windows only.
# Creates the pictures once (in %TEMP%), starts its own Gezik there in grid view with its
# own config folder, scrolls through the folder, and reports the private working set.
# Usage: scripts/perf/grid.ps1 [-Count 1000] [-Wait 8]
param([string]$Exe = "$PSScriptRoot\..\..\target\release\gezik.exe", [int]$Count = 1000, [int]$Wait = 8)

. "$PSScriptRoot\_window.ps1"
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms

$dir = Join-Path $env:TEMP "gezik-perf-grid-$Count"
if (-not (Test-Path $dir)) {
    New-Item -ItemType Directory $dir | Out-Null
    $bmp = New-Object System.Drawing.Bitmap 1600, 1200
    $g = [System.Drawing.Graphics]::FromImage($bmp)
    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $Count; $i++) {
        $g.Clear([System.Drawing.Color]::FromArgb(($i * 37) % 256, ($i * 91) % 256, ($i * 53) % 256))
        $g.FillEllipse([System.Drawing.Brushes]::White, ($i % 40) * 30, ($i % 30) * 30, 400, 300)
        $bmp.Save((Join-Path $dir ("photo{0:D4}.jpg" -f $i)), [System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat]::Jpeg)
    }
    $g.Dispose(); $bmp.Dispose()
}

$config = Join-Path $env:TEMP "gezik-perf-grid-config"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $config | Out-Null
Set-Content -Encoding utf8 (Join-Path $config "settings.toml") "[view]`nmode = `"grid`"`n"
$env:GEZIK_CONFIG_DIR = $config
$p = Start-Process $Exe -ArgumentList "`"$dir`"" -PassThru
try {
    $hwnd = Find-GezikWindow $p.Id 10000
    if ($hwnd -eq [IntPtr]::Zero) { throw "no Gezik window within 10 s" }
    Start-Sleep -Seconds $Wait
    # Page through the whole folder so every thumbnail is loaded once.
    [void][Win32]::SetForegroundWindow($hwnd)
    for ($i = 0; $i -lt 60; $i++) { [System.Windows.Forms.SendKeys]::SendWait("{PGDN}"); Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 150 }
    Start-Sleep -Seconds $Wait
    $mb = (Get-CimInstance Win32_PerfFormattedData_PerfProc_Process -Filter "IDProcess=$($p.Id)").WorkingSetPrivate / 1MB
    "grid, $Count pictures: Task Manager memory {0:N1} MB (target <= 50 MB)" -f $mb
} finally {
    Stop-Process -Id $p.Id -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Remove-Item Env:GEZIK_CONFIG_DIR -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
}
```

(`_window.ps1` bir `[Win32]` türü tanımlamıyorsa, betiğin başına `Add-Type -Namespace '' -Name Win32 -MemberDefinition '[DllImport("user32.dll")] public static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr h);'` ekle; tanımlıyorsa olduğu gibi kullan. Liste odağı açılışta zaten listededir.)

```markdown
## Görünüm sonrası (2026-10-04)

- macOS ve Linux'ta sistem ikonları yok (Gezik ikonları kullanılıyor); macOS'ta tür adı uzantıdan; iki sistemde de küçük resimler yalnızca Gezik'in çözdüğü png/jpeg/gif/webp/bmp. Linux tür adları (shared-mime-info) ve tarih biçimi denenmedi.
- Sütunlar sığmayınca yatay kaydırma yok (sağdaki sütunlar kırpılıyor); sütunları sürükleyerek sıralama ve çift tıkla otomatik genişlik yok.
- Seçili bir öğeye (çoklu seçim içinde) basmak seçimi hemen tek öğeye indiriyor; sürükle-bırak (Alt proje 4) için seçimin bırakmada daralması gerekecek.
- Ekran ölçeği değişince (pencereyi başka monitöre taşımak) ikon boyutu bir sonraki klasör gösterimine kadar eskisi kalıyor.
- Küçük resimlerin alfa kanalı önceden çarpılmış olabilir; saydam PNG küçük resimlerinde kenarlar koyu görünebilir.
- Windows önizleme işleyicileri (PDF/Office canlı görüntüleme) yok; sistem küçük resmi gösteriliyor.
- Çerçeve seçimi bir satırdan başlayıp o satır ekrandan çıkınca işaretçi yakalaması kaybolabiliyor; satırlar ve arka plan `move`/`up` olaylarını izleyerek telafi ediyor — farklı Slint sürümlerinde yeniden doğrulanmalı.
- Hızlı bakış penceresi ana pencerenin ortasında açılıyor; çok monitörlü kurulumlarda ekran dışına taşma kontrolü yok.
- Performans ölçümleri: (Task 16 Step 2 sayıları buraya).
```
